MISSING/UNCONFIRMED BODY CONVERSIONS — CURRENT PROFILE
CBBE-only does not automatically mean broken OBody. Usable CBBE TRI can morph female clothing; 3BA adds different weighting/sliders/physics support.
Humanoid body-slot models without a linked TRI cannot follow runtime body morphs through that model. Source family is unknown unless a matching project is inspected; vanilla/SOS is not guessed from a filename.
Creatures, obvious undead bodies, children, headwear, jewelry and fixed accessory projects are separate exceptions. No mod download/rebuild performed.
